REQUIREMENT 171: TRF-053

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 171

Clause / section / page: 4.5.1 / Stringers within the ice belt / 21-22
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-053-20260915T131504162210Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The section modulus of a stringer situated within the ice belt shall be calculated using Z = f_6*f_7*p*h*l^2/(m*sigma_y)*10^6 [cm^3] (4.11), and the effective shear area shall be A = sqrt(3)*f_6*f_7*f_8*p*h*l/(2*sigma_y)*10^4 [cm^2] (4.12). Use p from 4.2.2, h from 4.2.1, p*h = 0.15 MN/m when the calculated product is less than 0.15 MN/m, l as stringer span, m from 4.4.3, f_6 = 0.9, f_7 = 1.8, f_8 = 1.2, and sigma_y from 4.3.2.
Normalized requirement:
Set design_line_load_MN_m = max(p_MPa*h_m, 0.15). Compute Z_cm3 = 0.9*1.8*design_line_load_MN_m*l_m^2/(m*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^6 and A_cm2 = sqrt(3)*0.9*1.8*1.2*design_line_load_MN_m*l_m/(2*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^4, with m selected under 4.4.3.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF053IceStringerShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:iceStringer ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:node gen:IcePressureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one ice pressure in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceLoadHeight ;
        sh:node gen:DesignIceLoadHeightQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one design ice load height in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:span ;
        sh:node gen:SpanQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one span in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameMomentFactorM ;
        sh:node gen:FrameMomentFactorQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one dimensionless frame moment factor."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:node gen:YieldStrengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one yield strength in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designLineLoad ;
        sh:node gen:DesignLineLoadQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one calculated design line load in kN/m."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionModulus ;
        sh:node gen:SectionModulusQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one calculated section modulus in cm3."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:node gen:RequiredShearAreaQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ice stringer must provide exactly one calculated required shear area in cm2."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The design line load, section modulus, and required shear area must satisfy the TRF-053 formulae; derived decimal results are checked with a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-9." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:span ?spanNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?momentNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode ;
                      nltl:designLineLoad ?lineLoadNode ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?sectionModulusNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?shearAreaNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?pressure .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?height .
                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                ?momentNode qudt:numericValue ?moment .
                ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?yieldStrength .
                ?lineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?lineLoad .
                ?sectionModulusNode qudt:numericValue ?sectionModulus .
                ?shearAreaNode qudt:numericValue ?shearArea .

                BIND(IF((?pressure * ?height) < xsd:decimal("0.15"),
                        xsd:decimal("0.15"),
                        (?pressure * ?height)) AS ?designLineLoadMNPerM)

                BIND((?designLineLoadMNPerM * xsd:decimal("1000")) AS ?expectedLineLoad)
                BIND((xsd:decimal("0.9") * xsd:decimal("1.8") *
                      ?designLineLoadMNPerM * ?span * ?span /
                      (?moment * ?yieldStrength) * xsd:decimal("1000000"))
                     AS ?expectedSectionModulus)
                BIND((xsd:decimal("1.7320508075688772") * xsd:decimal("0.9") *
                      xsd:decimal("1.8") * xsd:decimal("1.2") *
                      ?designLineLoadMNPerM * ?span /
                      (xsd:decimal("2") * ?yieldStrength) *
                      xsd:decimal("10000"))
                     AS ?expectedShearArea)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?lineLoad - ?expectedLineLoad) >
                    IF(ABS(?expectedLineLoad) * xsd:decimal("0.000001") >
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"),
                       ABS(?expectedLineLoad) * xsd:decimal("0.000001"),
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"))
                    ||
                    ABS(?sectionModulus - ?expectedSectionModulus) >
                    IF(ABS(?expectedSectionModulus) * xsd:decimal("0.000001") >
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"),
                       ABS(?expectedSectionModulus) * xsd:decimal("0.000001"),
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"))
                    ||
                    ABS(?shearArea - ?expectedShearArea) >
                    IF(ABS(?expectedShearArea) * xsd:decimal("0.000001") >
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"),
                       ABS(?expectedShearArea) * xsd:decimal("0.000001"),
                       xsd:decimal("0.000000001"))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF053IceStringerShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:iceStringer ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:MegaPaQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceLoadHeight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:span ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameMomentFactorM ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:UnitlessQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:MegaPaQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designLineLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:KiloNewtonPerMetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionModulus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:CentiMetreCubedQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:CentiMetreSquaredQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The design line load, section modulus, and required shear area must satisfy the specified ice-stringer formulas within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute tolerance of 1e-6." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:span ?spanNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?momentFactorNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode ;
                      nltl:designLineLoad ?lineLoadNode ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?sectionModulusNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?shearAreaNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?l .
                ?momentFactorNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?sigma .
                ?lineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedLine .
                ?sectionModulusNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedZ .
                ?shearAreaNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedA .

                BIND(IF((?p * ?h) < 0.15, 0.15, (?p * ?h)) AS ?lineMNPerM)
                BIND((?lineMNPerM * 1000) AS ?expectedLine)
                BIND((0.9 * 1.8 * ?lineMNPerM * ?l * ?l / (?m * ?sigma) * 1000000) AS ?expectedZ)
                BIND((1.7320508075688772 * 0.9 * 1.8 * 1.2 * ?lineMNPerM * ?l / (2 * ?sigma) * 10000) AS ?expectedA)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?reportedLine - ?expectedLine) > (ABS(?expectedLine) * 0.000001 + 0.000001)
                    || ABS(?reportedZ - ?expectedZ) > (ABS(?expectedZ) * 0.000001 + 0.000001)
                    || ABS(?reportedA - ?expectedA) > (ABS(?expectedA) * 0.000001 + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:MegaPaQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA>
    ] .

gen:MetreQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
    ] .

gen:UnitlessQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
    ] .

gen:KiloNewtonPerMetreQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [

        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;

        sh:path qudt:unit ;

    ] .

    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF083Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:rotationalSpeedKnown ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must state whether the bollard propeller rotational speed is known."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:rotationalSpeedKnown ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerRotationalSpeedAtMaximumContinuousRatingBollard ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/REV-PER-MIN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The known bollard propeller speed at MCR must be provided in revolutions per minute."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:rotationalSpeedKnown ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerRotationalSpeedAtMaximumContinuousRatingBollard ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/REV-PER-MIN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The bollard propeller speed attributed from Table 6-9 must be provided in revolutions per minute."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:tableLookupCase ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:tableLookupApplied ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:lookupSelectionEvidence ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash9 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:tableReferenceValue
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "When the bollard propeller speed is unknown, a Table 6-9 lookup case and reference must be recorded."
```

```turtle
                sh:message "When the bollard propeller speed is unknown, a table lookup case using TRAFICOM Table 6-9 and selection evidence must be recorded."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix g: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

g:TRF004ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine which Ice Class Regulations apply."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Ships contracted on or after 1 January 2010 but before 1 January 2012 shall identify the 2008 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable regulation edition." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
                FILTER (
                    ?date >= "2010-01-01"^^xsd:date &&
                    ?date < "2012-01-01"^^xsd:date
                )
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                            nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2008 .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ?edition .
                        FILTER (?edition != nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2008)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF004_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine the applicable Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2008 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Ships contracted for construction from 1 January 2010 through 31 December 2011 must identify the 2008 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable regulation edition."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The 2008 Ice Class Regulations branch applies only to ships contracted for construction on or after 1 January 2010 and before 1 January 2012." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
                        FILTER (
                            !(
                                (
                                    YEAR(?date) > 2010 ||
                                    (YEAR(?date) = 2010 && MONTH(?date) >= 1 && DAY(?date) >= 1)
                                )
                                &&
                                YEAR(?date) < 2012
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The non-applicable branch is valid only when the construction contract date is outside the period from 1 January 2010 through 31 December 2011." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
                        FILTER (
                            (
                                (
                                    YEAR(?date) > 2010 ||
                                    (YEAR(?date) = 2010 && MONTH(?date) >= 1 && DAY(?date) >= 1)
                                )
                                &&
                                YEAR(?date) < 2012
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF004Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine whether the 2008 Ice Class Regulations apply."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ships contracted for construction on or after 1 January 2010 but before 1 January 2012 shall identify the 2008 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable regulation edition." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
    BIND(YEAR(?date) AS ?year)
    BIND(MONTH(?date) AS ?month)
    BIND(DAY(?date) AS ?day)
    FILTER(
        (?year > 2010 ||
         (?year = 2010 && (?month > 1 || (?month = 1 && ?day >= 1)))) &&
        (?year < 2012 ||
         (?year = 2012 && (?month < 1 || (?month = 1 && ?day < 1))))
    )
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
              nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2008
    }
}
""" ;
    ] .
```

```turtle
                      nltl:designVerticalIceForceAtBow ?fibQuantity ;
                      nltl:designVerticalIceShearForce ?fiQuantity ;
                      nltl:longitudinalDistributionFactor ?cfQuantity ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineLengthLUI ?luiQuantity .

                ?case nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ?positionQuantity ;
                      nltl:shearForceDirection nltl:positiveShearForce .

                ?fibQuantity qudt:numericValue ?fibValue .
                ?fiQuantity qudt:numericValue ?fiValue .
                ?cfQuantity qudt:numericValue ?cfValue .
                ?luiQuantity qudt:numericValue ?luiValue .
                ?positionQuantity qudt:numericValue ?positionValue .

                BIND((?cfValue * ?fibValue * "1000"^^xsd:decimal) AS ?expectedFi)

                FILTER (
                    ?positionValue < "0"^^xsd:decimal ||
                    ?positionValue > ?luiValue ||
                    (
                        !(
                            ?positionValue <= ("0.6"^^xsd:decimal * ?luiValue) &&
                            ?cfValue = "0"^^xsd:decimal
                        ) &&
                        !(
                            ?positionValue >= ("0.9"^^xsd:decimal * ?luiValue) &&
                            ?cfValue = "1"^^xsd:decimal
                        )
                    ) ||
                    ?fiValue != ?expectedFi
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-051-CalculationCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:calculationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shearForceDirection ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:DesignVerticalIceShearForceShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designVerticalIceForceAtBow ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designVerticalIceShearForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalDistributionFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:or (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minInclusive "0"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "0"^^xsd:decimal
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minInclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineLengthLUI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IceShearCalculationCaseShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For each positive-shear calculation case, the distribution factor must be 0.0 from the aft end through 0.6 LUI or 1.0 from 0.9 LUI through the forward end, and the design vertical ice shear force must equal Cf times FIB, with unit conversion from MN to kN." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:designVerticalIceForceAtBow ?fibNode ;
                      nltl:designVerticalIceShearForce ?fiNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalDistributionFactor ?cfNode ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineLengthLUI ?luiNode .

                ?case nltl:shearForceDirection nltl:positiveShearForce ;
                      nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ?positionNode .

                ?fibNode qudt:numericValue ?fib .
                ?fiNode qudt:numericValue ?fi .
                ?cfNode qudt:numericValue ?cf .
                ?luiNode qudt:numericValue ?lui .
                ?positionNode qudt:numericValue ?position .

                FILTER (
                    !(
                        (
                            ?position >= 0 &&
                            ?position <= (0.6 * ?lui) &&
                            ?cf = 0
                        ) ||
                        (
                            ?position >= (0.9 * ?lui) &&
                            ?position <= ?lui &&
                            ?cf = 1
                        )
                    )
                    ||
                    ABS(?fi - (?cf * ?fib * 1000)) >
                    (
                        0.000001 * ABS(?cf * ?fib * 1000)
                        +
                        0.000000001
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IceShearCalculationCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]

    sh:property [
```

```turtle
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_041_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:openingActuationType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must specify its opening actuation type."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hydraulicFluidFreezingOrViscosityControlPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "Hydraulic-fluid freezing or viscosity control presence must be a boolean when represented."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "If an opening or door is hydraulically operated, hydraulic-fluid freezing or excessive-viscosity control must be present and true." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:openingActuationType ?actuationType .
                FILTER (STR(?actuationType) = "hydraulic")
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hydraulicFluidFreezingOrViscosityControlPresent ?controlPresent .
                    FILTER (?controlPresent = true)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 178

Requirement ID: I2-031
Source family: I2
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: UR I2 Structural Requirements for Polar Class Ships
Source revision: Rev.4 Dec 2019
Clause / section / page: I2.6.1 / Framing - Local frames in bottom structures and transverse local frames in side structures / 12
Source URL: https://iacs.org.uk/resolutions/unified-requirements/ur-i/ur-i2-rev4-cln
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/ur-i2rev4.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 2142453d2965997a772f451982278e622413273048097924173216bd278df9a7
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-I2-031-20260915T095053166117Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The local frames in bottom structures (i.e. hull areas BIb, Mb and Sb) and transverse local frames in side structures are to be dimensioned such that the combined effects of shear and bending do not exceed the plastic strength of the member. The plastic strength is defined by the magnitude of midspan load that causes the development of a plastic collapse mechanism. For bottom structure the patch load shall be applied with the dimension (b) parallel with the frame direction.
Normalized requirement:
The local frames in bottom structures (i.e. hull areas BIb, Mb and Sb) and transverse local frames in side structures are to be dimensioned such that the combined effects of shear and bending do not exceed the plastic strength of the member. The plastic strength is defined by the magnitude of midspan load that causes the development of a plastic collapse mechanism. For bottom structure the patch load shall be applied with the dimension (b) parallel with the frame direction.

CASE I2-031-P01
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: Bottom local frame at capacity has correct patch orientation and demand equal to plastic strength.
Source clause: I2.6.1
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/i2-batch-f/I2-031-P01/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasStructuralMember ex:member .

ex:collapse a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 100.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

ex:demand a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 100.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

ex:loadcase a nltl:loadCase ;
    nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ex:demand ;
    nltl:loadPatchOrientation nltl:loadPatchHeightParallelToFrame ;
    nltl:patchLoad ex:patch .

ex:member a nltl:localFrame,
        nltl:structuralMember ;
    nltl:frameProfileType nltl:flatBarSection ;
    nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ex:loadcase ;
    nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ex:collapse ;
    nltl:plasticStrength ex:strength ;
    nltl:structuralLocation nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
    nltl:transverseFrameOrientation true .

ex:patch a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 50.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

ex:strength a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 100.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .


```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-ApplicableLocalMemberShape> ;
        sh:message "Applicable local frames in bottom structures and transverse local frames in side structures must satisfy the local-frame requirement."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-ApplicableLocalMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:class nltl:localFrame ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
            ] ;
            sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-StructuralMemberShape> ;
        ]
        [
            sh:class nltl:localFrame ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:transverseFrameOrientation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
            ] ;
            sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-StructuralMemberShape> ;
        ]
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:class nltl:localFrame ;
            ] ;
        ]
    ) .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-StructuralMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
            ]
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
            ]
        ) ;
        sh:message "The structural member location must be bottom structure or side structure."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-LoadCaseShape> ;
        sh:message "Each applicable structural member must have a load case."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-KiloNewtonQuantityShape> ;
        sh:message "Each applicable structural member must provide its midspan plastic collapse load in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plasticStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-KiloNewtonQuantityShape> ;
        sh:message "Each applicable structural member must provide its plastic strength in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:loadPatchOrientation ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:loadPatchHeightParallelToFrame ;
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:transverseFrameOrientation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Combined shear and bending demand must not exceed plastic strength, and plastic strength must equal the midspan plastic collapse load." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                ?demandQuantity q:numericValue ?demand .
                $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                ?strengthQuantity q:numericValue ?strength .
                $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?midspanQuantity .
                ?midspanQuantity q:numericValue ?midspan .
                FILTER (
                    ?demand > ?strength
                    ||
                    ?strength != ?midspan
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The midspan plastic collapse load must be the magnitude of load causing development of the plastic collapse mechanism; it must equal the member plastic strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?midspanQuantity .
                ?midspanQuantity q:numericValue ?mechanismDevelopmentLoad .
                $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                ?strengthQuantity q:numericValue ?plasticStrengthValue .
                FILTER (?mechanismDevelopmentLoad != ?plasticStrengthValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-LoadCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-QuantityValueShape> ;
        sh:message "Each load case must provide the combined shear and bending demand."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchOrientation ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:message "A load case load-patch orientation must be represented by a canonical value."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-QuantityValueShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path q:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path q:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-031-KiloNewtonQuantityShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path q:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path q:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_031_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:patchLoad ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the patch load in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plasticCollapseMechanism ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify the plastic collapse mechanism."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_StructuralMemberShape ;
        sh:message "Each represented structural member must satisfy the local-frame requirement."
    ] .

gs:I2_031_StructuralMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
            ]
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
            ]
        ) ;
        sh:message "The structural member must be identified as a bottom-structure or side-structure member."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plasticStrength ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The structural member must provide its plastic strength in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The structural member must provide the midspan load producing plastic collapse in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_LoadCaseShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The structural member must have a load case with the required shear and bending demand."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:bottomStructureLocation ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:loadPatchOrientation ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:loadPatchHeightParallelToFrame ;
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The plastic strength must equal the midspan plastic-collapse load within the permitted numerical tolerance." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?midspanQuantity .
                ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue .
                ?midspanQuantity qudt:numericValue ?midspanValue .
                FILTER (
                    ABS(?strengthValue - ?midspanValue)
                    >
                    (1e-6 * ABS(?midspanValue) + 1e-9)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:I2_031_LoadCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ;
        sh:node gs:I2_031_ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The load case must provide the combined shear and bending demand in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The combined shear and bending demand must not exceed the member plastic strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase $this .
                ?member nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue .
                FILTER (?demandValue > ?strengthValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:I2_031_ForceQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The force quantity must have one decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The force quantity must use the kN unit."
    ] .

```

REQUIREMENT 179: IMO-021

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 179
Batch: BATCH_18
Requirement ID: IMO-021
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 1.5 / Part I-A Chapter 1 - Operational assessment / 15
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-021-20260915T103322816331Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
In order to establish procedures or operational limitations, an assessment of the ship and its equipment shall be carried out, taking into consideration the following: the anticipated range of operating and environmental conditions, such as operation in low air temperature, operation in ice, operation in high latitude, and potential for abandonment onto ice or land; hazards, as listed in section 3 of the Introduction, as applicable; and additional hazards, if identified.
Normalized requirement:
An operational assessment shall exist and cover low-air-temperature operation, ice operation, high-latitude operation, abandonment onto ice or land, applicable Introduction section 3 hazards, and any identified additional hazards.
Required inputs: Operational assessment and hazard-coverage checklist

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF032Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gs:TRF032IceStrengthenedBranch
        gs:TRF032NotIceStrengthenedBranch
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the ship is not ice strengthened and the regulation-required scantling is less than the classification-society-required scantling, the selected design scantling must equal the classification-society-required scantling after unit normalization. Unit normalization must use identical units or complete, quantity-kind-compatible conversion metadata." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus false ;
                      nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ?regulationScantling ;
                      nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ?classificationScantling .

                ?regulationScantling qudt:numericValue ?regulationValue ;
                                     qudt:unit ?regulationUnit .
                ?classificationScantling qudt:numericValue ?classificationValue ;
                                          qudt:unit ?classificationUnit .

                OPTIONAL {
                    ?regulationUnit qudt:hasQuantityKind ?regulationKind .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    ?regulationUnit qudt:conversionMultiplier ?regulationMultiplier .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    ?regulationUnit qudt:conversionOffset ?regulationOffset .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    ?classificationUnit qudt:hasQuantityKind ?classificationKind .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    ?classificationUnit qudt:conversionMultiplier ?classificationMultiplier .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    ?classificationUnit qudt:conversionOffset ?classificationOffset .
                }

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?regulationValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?classificationValue) = xsd:decimal
                )

                BIND((?regulationUnit = ?classificationUnit) AS ?sameRequiredUnit)

                BIND((
                    ?sameRequiredUnit ||
                    (
                        BOUND(?regulationKind) &&
                        BOUND(?classificationKind) &&
                        ?regulationKind = ?classificationKind &&
                        BOUND(?regulationMultiplier) &&
                        BOUND(?regulationOffset) &&
                        BOUND(?classificationMultiplier) &&
                        BOUND(?classificationOffset)
                    )
                ) AS ?requiredNormalizationValid)

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        ?sameRequiredUnit,
                        ?regulationValue,
                        ?regulationValue * ?regulationMultiplier + ?regulationOffset
                    )
                    AS ?regulationNormalized
                )
                BIND(
                    IF(
                        ?sameRequiredUnit,
                        ?classificationValue,
                        ?classificationValue * ?classificationMultiplier + ?classificationOffset
                    )
                    AS ?classificationNormalized
                )

                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:selectedDesignScantling ?selectedScantling .
                    ?selectedScantling qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue ;
                                        qudt:unit ?selectedUnit .

                    FILTER (datatype(?selectedValue) = xsd:decimal)

                    OPTIONAL {
                        ?selectedUnit qudt:hasQuantityKind ?selectedKind .
                    }
                    OPTIONAL {
                        ?selectedUnit qudt:conversionMultiplier ?selectedMultiplier .
                    }
                    OPTIONAL {
                        ?selectedUnit qudt:conversionOffset ?selectedOffset .
                    }

                    BIND((?selectedUnit = ?classificationUnit) AS ?sameSelectedUnit)

                    BIND((
                        ?sameSelectedUnit ||
                        (
                            BOUND(?selectedKind) &&
                            BOUND(?classificationKind) &&
                            ?selectedKind = ?classificationKind &&
                            BOUND(?selectedMultiplier) &&
                            BOUND(?selectedOffset) &&
                            BOUND(?classificationMultiplier) &&
                            BOUND(?classificationOffset)
                        )
                    ) AS ?selectedNormalizationValid)

                    FILTER (?selectedNormalizationValid)

                    BIND(
                        IF(
                            ?sameSelectedUnit,
                            ?selectedValue,
                            ?selectedValue * ?selectedMultiplier + ?selectedOffset
                        )
                        AS ?selectedNormalized
                    )

                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:selectedDesignScantling ?otherSelectedScantling .
                        FILTER (?otherSelectedScantling != ?selectedScantling)
                    }
                }

                FILTER (
                    !?requiredNormalizationValid ||
                    (
                        BOUND(?regulationNormalized) &&
                        BOUND(?classificationNormalized) &&
                        ?regulationNormalized < ?classificationNormalized &&
                        (
                            !BOUND(?selectedScantling) ||
                            !BOUND(?selectedValue) ||
                            !BOUND(?selectedUnit) ||
                            !BOUND(?selectedNormalized) ||
                            ?selectedNormalized != ?classificationNormalized
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:TRF032IceStrengthenedBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:TRF032NotIceStrengthenedBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedDesignScantling ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-032-ScantlingSelectionShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "When the ship is not ice strengthened and the regulation-required scantling is less than the classification-society-required scantling, the selected design scantling must equal the classification-society-required scantling." ;
                sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ?regulationQuantity .
    ?regulationQuantity qudt:numericValue ?regulationValue .
    $this nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ?classificationQuantity .
    ?classificationQuantity qudt:numericValue ?classificationValue .
    FILTER (?regulationValue < ?classificationValue)
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:selectedDesignScantling ?selectedQuantity .
        ?selectedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .
        FILTER (?selectedValue = ?classificationValue)
    }
}
"""
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
    ) .
</urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-032-ScantlingSelectionShape>

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF032_Static a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ice strengthening status is required to determine applicability of the scantling rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The scantling derived from the regulations is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The classification-society-required scantling is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedDesignScantling ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The selected design scantling is required."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "When the ship is not ice strengthened and the regulation-derived scantling is less than the classification-society requirement, the selected design scantling must use the classification-society requirement." ;
                sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:iceStrengtheningStatus false ;
          nltl:regulationRequiredScantling ?regulation ;
          nltl:classificationSocietyRequiredScantling ?classification .
    ?regulation qudt:numericValue ?regulationValue .
    ?classification qudt:numericValue ?classificationValue .
    FILTER (?regulationValue < ?classificationValue)
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:selectedDesignScantling ?selected .
        ?selected qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .
        FILTER (?selectedValue = ?classificationValue)
    }
}
"""
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
